# rq6-loss-dev

RQ6, the loss on development data, three seeds each: GLiNER fine-tuned with binary
cross-entropy, the recall-weighted loss and focal loss, each trained with the config's
seed and with --seed 1 and --seed 2, against GLiNER zero-shot. Cross-entropy is also
scored at threshold 0.125, the threshold at which a calibrated cross-entropy model
reports what the weighted loss (weight 3) reports at 0.3: if the weighted loss only beats
cross-entropy at 0.3, it has only moved the threshold. Train the nine models first
(experiments/README.md, Training).

Stage: dev · commit e44e907302 · 2026-10-12T09:24:20+00:00 · seed 20261010, 1000 resamples · models: mdeberta-v3-base-tokenizer @ a0484667b2, gliner-multi-v2.1 @ 443d26d654, gliner-cs-sk-ce @ ee7c5fb9d2, gliner-cs-sk-ce-s1 @ 41418e7b74, gliner-cs-sk-ce-s2 @ 90e2adaf08, gliner-cs-sk-weighted @ 8a9742a93d, gliner-cs-sk-weighted-s1 @ 113de09f06, gliner-cs-sk-weighted-s2 @ c0ac251b10, gliner-cs-sk-focal @ a9c8ebc1ab, gliner-cs-sk-focal-s1 @ f734df43e9, gliner-cs-sk-focal-s2 @ de9cfe79fc · tool 0.5.0
